########################################
# Cosmo General Events
#
# 
########################################

namespace = cosmo_colonial_events

# Build a brothel 
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.1
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.1.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.1.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture

	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 			
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market_II
			}
				save_event_target_as = exportprovince
			}			
		}
		}


	option = {			# We will exploit this!
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.1.a
		trigger = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market_II
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_west_african_slaves_captured		
		}
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 			
			has_building = send_slaves_building
			NOT = {
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_building = brothel_district
			}
			}
			add_unrest = 1
			add_base_production = 1	
			add_province_modifier = {
			name = cosmo_west_africa_slaves_sent
			duration = -1  
			desc = cosmo_west_africa_slaves_sent_DESC
		}
		remove_building = send_slaves_building
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 			
			has_building = send_slaves_building
				OR = {
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_building = brothel_district
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_province_modifier = west_african_prostitutes
				}
				add_province_modifier = {
					name = west_african_prostitutes
					duration = 13250  
					desc = west_african_prostitutes_DESC
				}				
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = west_african_prostitutes
					}
				}
				add_province_modifier = {
					name = west_african_slave_prostitutes
					duration = 2160  
					desc = west_african_slave_prostitutes_DESC
				}				
			}			
			remove_building = send_slaves_building
		}		
		event_target:exportprovince = {
		remove_province_modifier = cosmo_west_african_slaves_captured
		add_base_production = -1	
		remove_building = cosmo_slave_market_II		
		}		

		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
				which = cosmoPleasureCounter
				value = 20
				}
		}		
	}
	option = {			# We will exploit this!

				}
				add_province_modifier = {
					name = japanese_prostitutes
					duration = 13250  
					desc = japanese_prostitutes_DESC
				}				
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = japanese_prostitutes
					}
				}
				add_province_modifier = {
					name = japan_slave_prostitutes
					duration = 2160

				}				
			}

		}

		add_base_production = -1	
		remove_building = cosmo_slave_market_II		
		}		

		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
				which = cosmoPleasureCounter
				value = 20
				}
		}		
	}

					name = russian_slave_prostitutes
					duration = 2160  
					desc = russian_slave_prostitutes_DESC
				}				
			}

		}

		remove_province_modifier = cosmo_russian_slaves_captured
		add_base_production = -1	
		remove_building = cosmo_slave_market_II		
		}

		change_variable = {
				which = cosmoPleasureCounter
				value = 20
				}
		}		
	}

# Build a brothel 
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.11
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.11.d
	picture = COLONIZATION_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		has_country_flag = cosmo_transfer_culture
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_transfer_culture
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_flag = colonist_exporter
			}
				save_event_target_as = exportprovince
			}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 			
			has_province_flag = colonist_import
			}
				save_event_target_as = importprovince
			}
		random_list = {
			60 = {
				set_country_flag = cosmo_no_conversion
			}
			40 = {
				set_country_flag = cosmo_conversion
			}			
		}							
	}
	}


	option = {			# We will exploit this!
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.11.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_conversion
			}
		country_event = {
		id = cosmo_colonial_events.12
		}			
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_no_conversion
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = colonist_exporter
				}
				clr_province_flag = colonist_exporter
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = colonist_import
				}
				clr_province_flag = colonist_import
			}			
		}		
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_conversion
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_no_conversion
	}		
}

# colony convert 
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.12
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.12.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.12.d
	picture = COLONIZATION_eventPicture
	goto = importprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_transfer_culture
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_flag = colonist_exporter
			}
				save_event_target_as = exportprovince
			}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 			
			has_province_flag = colonist_import
			}
				save_event_target_as = importprovince
			}							
	}
	}


	option = {			# We will exploit this!
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.12.a
		event_target:importprovince = {
			change_culture = event_target:exportprovince						
		}
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = colonist_exporter
			}
			clr_province_flag = colonist_exporter
		}
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = colonist_import
			}
			clr_province_flag = colonist_import
		}		
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_conversion
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_no_conversion
	}
	option = {			# We will exploit this!
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.12.b
		event_target:importprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_no_culture_change
				duration = 1825  
			}
		}		
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = colonist_exporter
			}
			clr_province_flag = colonist_exporter
		}
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = colonist_import
			}
			clr_province_flag = colonist_import
		}		
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_conversion
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_no_conversion
	}			
}

	id = cosmo_colonial_events.202

	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.202.d

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		any_owned_province = {
					OR = {
					superregion = africa_superregion
					superregion = southern_africa_superregion
					}
			has_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}
	}

		days = 1
	}

		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_colony_needwomen
			random_owned_province = {
				limit = {
							OR = {
					superregion = africa_superregion
					superregion = southern_africa_superregion
					}
					has_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen		
				}
				save_event_target_as = colonyprovince
				set_province_flag = cosmo_stolenwomen
				clr_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
				}			
		}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.202.a
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_scaled_local_mil_power = 10 
			add_nationalism = 10

				name = native_unrest
				duration =  7300 
				}
			set_province_flag = cosmo_stolenwomen
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.202.b
		create_conquistador = { tradition = 80 } 
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_nationalism = 10 
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = native_unrest
				duration =  7300 
				}
			set_province_flag = cosmo_stolenwomen
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.202.c
		create_explorer = { tradition = 80 }  
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_nationalism = 10 
			add_province_modifier = {

			}
	}

	id = cosmo_colonial_events.203
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.203.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.203.d
	picture = cosmogfx_AFRICAN_NUDE_PROSTITUTE_eventPicture
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		any_owned_province = {
			has_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes
			}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

		immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_colony_needwomen
		}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.203.a
		random_owned_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes		
			}
			add_base_production = 4
			center_of_trade = 1 
			set_province_flag = cosmo_brothel
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes
			}
	}		
	option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.203.b
		change_price = {
		trade_goods = prostitutes
		key = imported_prostitutes
		value = 0.25
		duration = 7300
		} 
		random_owned_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes		
			}
			
		hidden_effect = {
			set_province_flag = cosmo_brothel
			set_province_flag = cosmo_sentwomen
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes
			
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_colonial_events.204
			days = 50
			}
		}
	}
}

						#set_province_flag = charruan_colony
						change_culture = charruan
					}
				}
				guarani_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = guarani_colony
						change_culture = guarani
					}
				}			
				tupinamba_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = tupinamba_colony
						change_culture = tupinamba
					}
				}
				ge_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = ge_colony
						change_culture = ge
					}
				}												
			}			
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_culture_change
			clr_province_flag = guajiro_colony	
			clr_province_flag = muisca_colony	
			clr_province_flag = jivaro_colony	
			clr_province_flag = inca_colony	
			clr_province_flag = aimara_colony	
			clr_province_flag = diaguita_colony	
			clr_province_flag = mapuche_colony	
			clr_province_flag = patagonian_colony	
			clr_province_flag = het_colony	
			clr_province_flag = huarpe_colony	
			clr_province_flag = charruan_colony	
			clr_province_flag = guarani_colony	
			clr_province_flag = tupinamba_colony	
			clr_province_flag = ge_colony			
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry
			}
		}
	}	
}

# Colony SA Steal women
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.212
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.212.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.212.d
	picture = cosmogfx_NATIVE_NUDE_TERRORIZED_eventPicture
	goto = colonyprovince
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		any_owned_province = {
			has_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

		immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_colony_needwomen
		random_owned_province = {
			limit = {
				superregion = south_america_superregion			
				has_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen		
			}
			save_event_target_as = colonyprovince
			set_province_flag = cosmo_stolenwomen
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}			
		}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.212.a
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_scaled_local_mil_power = 10 
			add_nationalism = 10 
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = native_unrest
				duration =  7300 
				}
			set_province_flag = cosmo_stolenwomen
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.212.b
		create_conquistador = { tradition = 80 } 
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_nationalism = 10 
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = native_unrest
				duration =  7300 
				}
			set_province_flag = cosmo_stolenwomen
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.212.c
		create_explorer = { tradition = 80 }  
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_nationalism = 10 
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = native_unrest
				duration =  7300 
				}
			set_province_flag = cosmo_stolenwomen
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}
	}			
}

# Colony SA Brothel
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.213
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.213.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.213.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_CELEBRATE_eventPicture
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		any_owned_province = {
			has_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes
			}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

		immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_colony_needwomen
		}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.213.a
		random_owned_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes		
			}
			add_base_production = 4
			center_of_trade = 1 
			set_province_flag = cosmo_brothel
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes
			}
	}		
	option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.213.b
	change_price = {
		trade_goods = prostitutes
		key = imported_prostitutes
		value = 0.25
		duration = 7300
		} 
		random_owned_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes		
			}
			
		hidden_effect = {
			set_province_flag = cosmo_brothel
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes
		}
		}
	}
}

				}
				clr_province_flag = cosmo_colony_culture
			}

		which = cosmoSAColonyCounter
		value = 20
		}

		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAColonyCounter	
			value = 100
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAColonyCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}			
			}
	}

	goto = colonyprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		any_owned_province = {
					superregion = north_america_superregion			
			has_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry
				NOT = {
					is_colony = yes

# Colony NA Steal women
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.222
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.222.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.222.d
	picture = cosmogfx_NA_NUDE_RAID_eventPicture
	goto = colonyprovince
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		any_owned_province = {
			has_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

		immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_colony_needwomen
		random_owned_province = {
			limit = {
				superregion = north_america_superregion			
				has_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen		
			}
			save_event_target_as = colonyprovince
			set_province_flag = cosmo_stolenwomen
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}			
		}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.222.a
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_scaled_local_mil_power = 10 
			add_nationalism = 10 
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = native_unrest
				duration =  7300 
				}
			set_province_flag = cosmo_stolenwomen
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.222.b
		create_conquistador = { tradition = 80 } 
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_nationalism = 10 
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = native_unrest
				duration =  7300 
				}
			set_province_flag = cosmo_stolenwomen
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.222.c
		create_explorer = { tradition = 80 }  
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_nationalism = 10 
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = native_unrest
				duration =  7300 
				}
			set_province_flag = cosmo_stolenwomen
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_stealwomen
			}
	}			
}

# Colony NA Brothel
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.223
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.223.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.223.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_CELEBRATE_eventPicture
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		any_owned_province = {
			has_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes
			}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

		immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_colony_needwomen
		}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.223.a
		random_owned_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes		
			}
			add_base_production = 4
			center_of_trade = 1 
			set_province_flag = cosmo_brothel
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes
			}
	}		
	option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.223.b
	change_price = {
		trade_goods = prostitutes
		key = imported_prostitutes
		value = 0.25
		duration = 7300
		} 
		random_owned_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes		
			}
			
		hidden_effect = {
			set_province_flag = cosmo_brothel
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_prostitutes
		}
		}
	}
}

			limit = {
				any_owned_province = {
					has_province_flag = cosmo_colony_culture
					AND = {
						region = central_america_region
						NOT = {
							province_id = 836 #central
							province_id = 2663
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_ruler_flag = miskito_colony
		}											
			}
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.230.a
		add_dip_power = -70
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_colonysize = 200
			change_native_size = 20
			add_base_manpower = 2
			set_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry
		}
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
				miskito_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						set_province_flag = miskito_colony
						set_province_flag = cosmo_culture_change
						change_culture = miskito
					}
				}												
				arawak_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						set_province_flag = arawak_colony
						set_province_flag = cosmo_culture_change
						change_culture = arawak
					}
				}			
				carib_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						set_province_flag = carib_colony
						set_province_flag = cosmo_culture_change
						change_culture = carib
					}
				}
				muisca_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						set_province_flag = muisca_colony
						set_province_flag = cosmo_culture_change
						change_culture = muisca
					}
				}
			}		
			clr_ruler_flag = miskito_colony	
			clr_ruler_flag = arawak_colony	
			clr_ruler_flag = carib_colony	
			clr_ruler_flag = muisca_colony		
			hidden_effect = {
			every_owned_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = cosmo_colony_culture

			}				
			# country_event = {
			# id = cosmo_colonial_events.231

		which = cosmoSAColonyCounter
		value = 20
		}

		limit = {
			check_variable = {

			value = 180
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAColonyCounter

			}

# Colony Carib and CAIntermarry
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.231
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.231.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.231.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SA_NUDE_WIVES_eventPicture
	goto = colonyprovince
		
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		any_owned_province = {
			superregion = central_america_superregion			
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry
			NOT = {
				is_colony = yes 
			}			
			}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

		immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_colony_needwomen
			random_province = {
				limit = {
					superregion = central_america_superregion			
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
					has_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry		
				}
				save_event_target_as = colonyprovince
				set_province_flag = cosmo_mixedpop
				clr_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry
				}			
		}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.231.a
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_base_production = 1
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_culture_change
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_province_flag
				miskito_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = miskito_colony
						change_culture = miskito
					}
				}												
				arawak_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = arawak_colony
						change_culture = arawak
					}
				}			
				carib_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = carib_colony
						change_culture = carib
					}
				}
				muisca_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = muisca_colony
						change_culture = muisca
					}
				}
			}
			clr_province_flag = miskito_colony	
			clr_province_flag = arawak_colony	
			clr_province_flag = carib_colony	
			clr_province_flag = muisca_colony						
			}
	}		
	option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.231.b
		create_conquistador = { tradition = 80 } 
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_province_flag
				miskito_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = miskito_colony
						change_culture = miskito
					}
				}												
				arawak_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = arawak_colony
						change_culture = arawak
					}
				}			
				carib_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = carib_colony
						change_culture = carib
					}
				}
				muisca_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = muisca_colony
						change_culture = muisca
					}
				}
			}			
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_culture_change
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry
			clr_province_flag = miskito_colony	
			clr_province_flag = arawak_colony	
			clr_province_flag = carib_colony	
			clr_province_flag = muisca_colony				
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.231.c
		create_explorer = { tradition = 80 }  
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_province_flag
				miskito_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = miskito_colony
						change_culture = miskito
					}
				}												
				arawak_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = arawak_colony

					}
				}			
				carib_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = carib_colony

					}
				}
				muisca_colony = {
					event_target:colonyprovince = {
						#set_province_flag = muisca_colony

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.241
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.241.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.241.d
	picture = cosmogfx_ASIAN_PREGNANT_NUDE_eventPicture
	goto = colonyprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		any_owned_province = {
			OR = {
				superregion = east_indies_superregion
				region = australia_region
			}
			NOT = {
				is_colony = yes 
			}
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry
			}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

		immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_colony_needwomen
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			OR = {
				superregion = east_indies_superregion
				region = australia_region
			}
				has_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry		
			NOT = {
				is_colony = yes 
			}
			}
			save_event_target_as = colonyprovince
			set_province_flag = cosmo_mixedpop
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry
			}			
		}
	}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.241.a
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:colonyprovince = {
				has_province_flag = aboriginal_colony
			}				
			}
			event_target:colonyprovince = {
				change_culture = aboriginal
				clr_province_flag = aboriginal_colony
			}						
		}		
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
			add_base_production = 1
			set_province_flag = cosmo_mixedpop
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry
			}
	}

				change_culture = aboriginal
				clr_province_flag = cosmo_culture_change				
				clr_province_flag = aboriginal_colony
			}						
		}
		event_target:colonyprovince = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_province_flag = cosmo_mixedpop
			clr_province_flag = cosmo_intermarry
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = cosmo_colonial_events.241.c
		create_explorer = { tradition = 80 }

			limit = {
			event_target:colonyprovince = {

			}				
			}

# Privateer Enslavement
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.615
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.615.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.615.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_barbary_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {	
				culture_group = west_african			
				culture_group = sahelian			
				culture_group = mande			
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}					
				}
				OR = {	
				culture_group = west_african			
				culture_group = sahelian			
				culture_group = mande			
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = muslim
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = christian
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				}
			}			
				sea_zone = { save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince }			
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
						has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
						NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }					
						has_port = yes 			
					owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}
						
					}
			OR = {	
				culture_group = west_african			
				culture_group = sahelian			
				culture_group = mande			
			}
				NOT = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				owner = {
					alliance_with = ROOT
				}		
				}							
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_slaves_taken
				duration =  3650 
				}			
		}	
		}
	}

	name = cosmo_colonial_events.615.a 	
	trigger = {

# Devastation Enslavement
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.616
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.616.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.616.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince

	trigger = {
		ai = no	
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_thai_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_barbary_slave_market		
		}
		any_known_country = {
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 30
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
			OR = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
				save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince
			}
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.616.a 
		trigger = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}							
		}			
		}
		event_target:enslavedprovince = {									
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_slaves_taken
			duration =  360 
			

			}	
			}
		if = {
			limit = {
	NOT = {		
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			OR = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
		}			
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_thai_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
			}
		}
		random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_thai_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		add_base_production = 1
		add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_thai_slaves_captured
				duration = 3650
				desc = cosmo_thai_slaves_captured_DESC
	
		}
			}
		add_adm_power = -25
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			OR = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
		}			
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_thai_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
			}
		random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			OR = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_thai_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		add_base_production = 1
		add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_thai_slaves_captured
				duration = 3650
				desc = cosmo_thai_slaves_captured_DESC
	
		}
			}
		}			
	}
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.616.b 
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_barbary_slave_market
	}
		event_target:enslavedprovince = {
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_slaves_taken
			duration =  360 
			

			}
			owner = {
				add_opinion = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
				}
			}	
			}	
		add_treasury = 150		
		event_target:enslavedprovince = {
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_slaves_taken
			duration =  360 
			

			}	
			}		
			random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			}
		add_base_production = 1 
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_thai_slaves_captured
			duration =  365 
			desc = cosmo_thai_slaves_captured

			}
			}
	}
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.616.7 
		event_target:enslavedprovince = {
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_slaves_taken
			duration =  360 
			

			}	
			}		
	}

# Privateer Enslavement indochina
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.617
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.617.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.617.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_sulu_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}

		days = 1
	}

	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {

				limit = {

				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = christian
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				}
			}			
				sea_zone = { save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince }			
			}

						has_port = yes

						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}
						
					}
			OR = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
				NOT = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				owner = {
					alliance_with = ROOT
				}		
				}							
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_slaves_taken
				duration =  3650 
				}			
		}	
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.617.a

			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market

# Devastation Enslavement
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.618
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.618.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.618.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince

	trigger = {
		ai = no	
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_malay_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_sulu_slave_market		
		}
		any_known_country = {
				any_owned_province = { 
			superregion = east_indies_superregion
			NOT = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 30
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
			superregion = east_indies_superregion
			NOT = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
				save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince
			}
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.618.a 
		trigger = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}							
		}			
		}
		event_target:enslavedprovince = {									
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_slaves_taken
			duration =  360 
			

			}	
			}
		if = {
			limit = {
	NOT = {		
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			superregion = east_indies_superregion
			NOT = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
		}			
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_malay_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
			}
		}
		random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_malay_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		add_base_production = 1
		add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_malay_slaves_captured
				duration = 3650
				desc = cosmo_malay_slaves_captured_DESC
	
		}
			}
		add_adm_power = -25
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			superregion = east_indies_superregion
			NOT = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
		}			
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_malay_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
			}
		random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			superregion = east_indies_superregion
			NOT = {
				region = burma_region 
				region = indo_china_region
			}
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {

				desc = cosmo_malay_slaves_captured_DESC

# Privateer Enslavement Malay
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.619
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.619.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.619.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_sulu_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				region = malaya_region 
				region = indonesia_region
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}					
				}
				OR = {
				region = malaya_region 
				region = indonesia_region
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = muslim
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = christian
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				}
			}			
				sea_zone = { save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince }			
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
						has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
						NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }					
						has_port = yes 			
					owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}
						
					}
			OR = {
				region = malaya_region 
				region = indonesia_region
			}
				NOT = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				owner = {
					alliance_with = ROOT
				}		
				}							
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_slaves_taken
				duration =  3650 
				}			
		}	
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.619.a 	
	trigger = {

			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

# Devastation Enslavement Korea
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.622
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.622.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.622.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince

	trigger = {
		ai = no	
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_korea_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_sulu_slave_market		
		}
		any_known_country = {
				any_owned_province = { 
			region = korea_region

			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 30
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
			region = korea_region

			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
				save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.622.a 
		trigger = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}							
		}			
		}
		event_target:enslavedprovince = {									
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_slaves_taken
			duration =  360 
			

			}	
			}
		if = {
			limit = {
	NOT = {		
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			region = korea_region
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
		}			
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_korea_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
			}
		}
		random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_korea_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		add_base_production = 1
		add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_korea_slaves_captured
				duration = 3650
				desc = cosmo_korea_slaves_captured_DESC
	
		}
			}
		add_adm_power = -25
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			region = korea_region
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
		}			
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_korea_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
			}
		random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			region = korea_region
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_korea_slaves_captured

		add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_korea_slaves_captured
				duration = 3650
				desc = cosmo_korea_slaves_captured_DESC
	
		}
			}
		}		
	}

country_event = {

	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.623.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_sulu_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				region = korea_region
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}					
				}
				OR = {
				region = korea_region
			}

			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}

				limit = {
					religion_group = muslim
				}

					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = christian
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				}
			}			
				sea_zone = { save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince }			
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
						has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
						NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }					
						has_port = yes 			
					owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}
						
					}
			OR = {
				region = korea_region
			}
				NOT = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				owner = {
					alliance_with = ROOT
				}		
				}							
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_slaves_taken
				duration =  3650 
				}			
		}	
		}
	}

					has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
					has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
					}
			}
			owner = {
				custom_tooltip = cannot_sell_religion_tt			
			}
			}		
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
		}
		}
		add_treasury = 100				
			random_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
					OR = {
					has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
					has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
					has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
					}
			}
			add_base_production = 1 
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_korea_slaves_captured
				duration =  365 
				desc = cosmo_korea_slaves_captured_DESC
				}
			}		
	}
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim			
	}
	option = {

# Devastation Enslavement
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.624
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.624.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.624.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince

	trigger = {
		ai = no	
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_china_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_sulu_slave_market		
		}
		any_known_country = {
				any_owned_province = { 
			superregion = china_superregion
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 30
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
			superregion = china_superregion

			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
				save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.624.a 
		trigger = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}							
		}			
		}
		event_target:enslavedprovince = {									
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_slaves_taken
			duration =  360 
			

			}	
			}
		if = {
			limit = {
	NOT = {		
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			superregion = china_superregion
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
		}			
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_china_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
			}
		}
		random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_china_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		add_base_production = 1
		add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_china_slaves_captured
				duration = 3650
				desc = cosmo_china_slaves_captured_DESC
	
		}
			}
		add_adm_power = -25
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			superregion = china_superregion
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
		}			
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_china_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
			}
		random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			superregion = china_superregion
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_china_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		add_base_production = 1
		add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_china_slaves_captured
				duration = 3650
				desc = cosmo_china_slaves_captured_DESC
	
		}
			}
		}		
	}
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.624.b 
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_sulu_slave_market
	}
		add_years_of_income = 0.2	
		event_target:enslavedprovince = {
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_slaves_taken
			duration =  360 
			

			}	
			}		
			random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			}
		add_base_production = 1 
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_china_slaves_captured
			duration =  365 
			desc = cosmo_china_slaves_captured_DESC

			}
			}
	}
	option = {

		event_target:enslavedprovince = {
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_slaves_taken

# Privateer Enslavement China
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.625
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.625.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.625.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_sulu_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				superregion = china_superregion
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}					
				}
				OR = {
				superregion = china_superregion
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = muslim
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = christian
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				}
			}			
				sea_zone = { save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince }			
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
						has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
						NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }					
						has_port = yes 			
					owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}
						
					}
			OR = {
				superregion = china_superregion
			}
				NOT = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				owner = {
					alliance_with = ROOT
				}		
				}							
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_slaves_taken
				duration =  3650 
				}			
		}	
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.625.a

# Privateer Enslavement Japan
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.627
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.627.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.627.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_sulu_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				region = japan_region
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}					
				}
				OR = {
				region = japan_region
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = muslim
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = christian
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				}
			}			
				sea_zone = { save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince }			
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
						has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
						NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }					
						has_port = yes 			
					owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}
						
					}
			OR = {
				region = japan_region
			}
				NOT = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				owner = {
					alliance_with = ROOT
				}		
				}							
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_slaves_taken
				duration =  3650 
				}			
		}	
		}
	}

			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}

		}
		}

		}

			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				culture_group = apachean    #change to american cultures
				culture_group = otomanguean    #change to american cultures
				culture_group = maya    #change to american cultures
				culture_group = central_american     #change to american cultures
				culture_group = aridoamerican    #change to american cultures

			}

		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided

	}

# Devastation Enslavement
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.632

	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.632.d

	trigger = {
		ai = no	
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

			has_province_modifier = cosmo_south_american_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_barbary_slave_market		
		}
		any_known_country = {
				any_owned_province = { 
	OR = {		
		culture_group = andean_group   #change to american cultures

		culture_group = je    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = maranon    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = chibchan    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = araucanian     #change to american cultures
		culture_group = carribean      #change to american cultures
		culture_group = araucanian     #change to american cultures
		}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
		}

		days = 30

		culture_group = araucanian     #change to american cultures
		}

			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}

		NOT = {

# Privateer Enslavement south america
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.633
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.633.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.633.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market

			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market

		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_sulu_slave_market		
		}

			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				culture_group = andean_group   #change to american cultures
				culture_group = je_tupi    #change to american cultures
				culture_group = je    #change to american cultures
				culture_group = maranon    #change to american cultures
				culture_group = chibchan    #change to american cultures
				culture_group = araucanian     #change to american cultures

				culture_group = araucanian     #change to american cultures
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}

		days = 1

# Devastation Enslavement
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.634
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.634.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.634.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince

	trigger = {
		ai = no	
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_north_american_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_barbary_slave_market		
		}
		any_known_country = {
				any_owned_province = { 
	OR = {				
		culture_group = eskaleut    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = central_algonquian    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = plains_algonquian    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = eastern_algonquian    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = iroquoian    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = siouan    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = caddoan     #change to american cultures
		culture_group = muskogean     #change to american cultures
		culture_group = sonoran     #change to american cultures
		culture_group = na_dene     #change to american cultures
		culture_group = penutian      #change to american cultures
		}
		
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 30
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
	OR = {				
		culture_group = eskaleut    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = central_algonquian    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = plains_algonquian    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = eastern_algonquian    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = iroquoian    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = siouan    #change to american cultures
		culture_group = caddoan     #change to american cultures
		culture_group = muskogean     #change to american cultures
		culture_group = sonoran     #change to american cultures
		culture_group = na_dene     #change to american cultures
		culture_group = penutian      #change to american cultures
		}
		
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}

			}
						has_building = cosmo_slave_market

# Devastation Enslavement
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.636
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.636.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.636.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince

	trigger = {
		ai = no	
		NOT = {	
			religion_group = christian
			religion_group = anathanist
			}		
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_europe_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_barbary_slave_market		
		}
		any_known_country = {
				any_owned_province = { 
	OR = {				
				culture_group = germanic		
				culture_group = scandinavian		
				culture_group = british		
				culture_group = gaelic
				culture_group = latin
				culture_group = iberian		
				culture_group = french	
				culture_group = carpathian		
				culture_group = south_slavic		
				culture_group = west_slavic			
				culture_group = east_slavic		
				culture_group = baltic		
				culture_group = byzantine
		}
		
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 30
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
	OR = {				
				culture_group = germanic		
				culture_group = scandinavian		
				culture_group = british		
				culture_group = gaelic
				culture_group = latin
				culture_group = iberian		
				culture_group = french	
				culture_group = carpathian		
				culture_group = south_slavic		
				culture_group = west_slavic			
				culture_group = east_slavic		
				culture_group = baltic		
				culture_group = byzantine
		}
		
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
			controlled_by = ROOT
			owner = { has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
				devastation = 20
				development = 3
		NOT = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken
			any_neighbor_province = { 
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken			
			} 
			}
			}
				save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince
			}
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.636.a 
		trigger = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}							
		}			
		}
		event_target:enslavedprovince = {									
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_slaves_taken
			duration =  360 
			

			}	
			}
		if = {
			limit = {
	NOT = {		
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
	OR = {				
				superregion = europe_superregion
				superregion = eastern_europe_superregion
		}
		has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
		}			
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_europe_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
			}
		}
		random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_europe_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
		add_base_production = 1
		add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_europe_slaves_captured
				duration = 3650
				desc = cosmo_europe_slaves_captured_DESC
	
		}
			}
		add_adm_power = -25
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
	OR = {				
				superregion = europe_superregion
				superregion = eastern_europe_superregion
		}
		has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
		}			
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_europe_slaves_captured	
			}				
		}
			}
		random_province = {
		limit = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
	OR = {				
				superregion = europe_superregion
				superregion = eastern_europe_superregion
		}
		has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {	
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_europe_slaves_captured	
			}

		add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_europe_slaves_captured
				duration = 3650
				desc = cosmo_europe_slaves_captured_DESC
	
		}
			}
		}		
	}

				}
			}

		event_target:enslavedprovince = {
		add_province_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_slaves_taken
			duration =  360 
			

			}

country_event = {

	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_barbary_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				culture_group = iberian		
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {

				owner = {

				alliance_with = ROOT

				limit = {
					religion_group = christian

				}
			}

# Privateer Enslavement british
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.639
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.639.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.639.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_barbary_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				culture_group = british		
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}					
				}
				OR = {
				culture_group = british		
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = muslim
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = christian
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				}
			}			
				sea_zone = { save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince }			
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
						has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
						NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }					
						has_port = yes 			
					owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}
						
					}
			OR = {
				culture_group = british		
			}
				NOT = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				owner = {
					alliance_with = ROOT
				}		
				}							
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_slaves_taken
				duration =  3650 
				}			
		}	
		}
	}

# Privateer Enslavement french
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.643
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.643.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.643.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_barbary_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				culture_group = french		
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}					
				}
				OR = {
				culture_group = french		
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = muslim
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = christian
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				}
			}			
				sea_zone = { save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince }			
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
						has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
						NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }					
						has_port = yes 			
					owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}
						
					}
			OR = {
				culture_group = french		
			}
				NOT = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				owner = {
					alliance_with = ROOT
				}		
				}							
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_slaves_taken
				duration =  3650 
				}			
		}	
		}
	}

# Privateer Enslavement balkan
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.647
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.647.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.647.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_khanate_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				culture_group = south_slavic
				culture_group = carpathian
				culture_group = byzantine
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}					
				}
				OR = {
				culture_group = south_slavic
				culture_group = carpathian
				culture_group = byzantine
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = muslim
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = christian
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				}
			}			
				sea_zone = { save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince }			
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
						has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
						NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }					
						has_port = yes 			
					owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}
						
					}
			OR = {
				culture_group = south_slavic
				culture_group = carpathian
				culture_group = byzantine
			}
				NOT = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				owner = {
					alliance_with = ROOT
				}		
				}							
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_slaves_taken
				duration =  3650 
				}			
		}	
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.647.a 	
	trigger = {

			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
