# Privateer Enslavement italian
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.659
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.659.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.659.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_barbary_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			AND = {
				culture_group = latin
				NOT = {
				culture = venetian
			}
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}					
				}
				AND = {
				culture_group = latin
				NOT = {
				culture = venetian
			}
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = muslim
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = christian
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				}
			}			
				sea_zone = { save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince }			
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
						has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
						NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }					
						has_port = yes 			
					owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}
						
					}
			AND = {
				culture_group = latin
				NOT = {
				culture = venetian
			}
			}
				NOT = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				owner = {
					alliance_with = ROOT
				}		
				}							
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_slaves_taken
				duration =  3650 
				}			
		}	
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.659.a 	
	trigger = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}							
		}
		NOT = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				religion_group = muslim
			}
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				religion_group = christian
			}			
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				religion_group = muslim
			}
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				religion_group = christian
			}			
		}			
		}
		custom_tooltip = cannot_sell_religion_tt		
	}
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				religion_group = muslim
			}
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				religion_group = christian
			}			
		}				
				NOT = {
					any_province = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			AND = {
				culture_group = latin
				NOT = {
				culture = venetian
			}
			}
						has_building = cosmo_slave_market
						NOT = {				
							has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
						}			
						NOT = {	
							has_province_modifier = cosmo_italian_slaves_captured	
							}												
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					any_province = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
						has_building = cosmo_slave_market
						NOT = {				
							has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
						}			
						NOT = {	
							has_province_modifier = cosmo_italian_slaves_captured	
							}												
					}

						NOT = {				
							has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
						}															
					}								
			}
			random_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
					has_building = cosmo_slave_market
			culture = venetian							
						NOT = {				
							has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
						}
				}
				add_base_production = 1
				add_province_modifier = { 
						name = cosmo_italian_slaves_captured
						duration = 3650
						desc = cosmo_italian_slaves_captured_DESC
			
				}
			}
			add_adm_power = -25			
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				religion_group = muslim
			}
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				religion_group = christian
			}			
		}				
				NOT = {
					any_province = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
			AND = {
				culture_group = latin
				NOT = {
				culture = venetian
			}
			}
						has_building = cosmo_slave_market
						NOT = {				
							has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
						}			
						NOT = {	
							has_province_modifier = cosmo_italian_slaves_captured	
							}												
					}
				}
					any_province = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT	
						has_building = cosmo_slave_market
						NOT = {				
							has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
						}			
						NOT = {	
							has_province_modifier = cosmo_italian_slaves_captured	
							}												
					}								
			}
			random_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
					has_building = cosmo_slave_market
						NOT = {				
							has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
						}
			culture = venetian								
						NOT = {	
							has_province_modifier = cosmo_italian_slaves_captured	
							}						
				}
				add_base_production = 1
				add_province_modifier = { 
						name = cosmo_italian_slaves_captured
						duration = 3650
						desc = cosmo_italian_slaves_captured_DESC

# Privateer Enslavement east african
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.655
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.655.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.655.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
	OR = {	
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
			has_building = cosmo_slave_market								
		NOT = {				
			has_province_flag = cosmo_barbary_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_sulu_slave_market
			has_province_flag = cosmo_horn_slave_market
		}
		}
		has_country_flag = cosmo_owns_barbary_slave_market		
		}	
		any_known_country = {	
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = slaves_raided
			}				
				any_owned_province = { 
			OR = {
				culture_group = african
				culture_group = cushitic
				culture_group = sudanese
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
		}
		}		
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}					
				}
				OR = {
				culture_group = african
				culture_group = cushitic
				culture_group = sudanese
			}
			NOT = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			owner = {
				alliance_with = ROOT
			}		
			}			
		has_port = yes 			
		has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
		NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = muslim
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_muslim
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					religion_group = christian
				}
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = cosmo_enslaving_christian
				}
			}			
				sea_zone = { save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince }			
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
						has_province_modifier = slaves_raided
						NOT = { has_province_modifier = cosmo_slaves_taken }					
						has_port = yes 			
					owner = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = slaves_raided
					}
						
					}
			OR = {
				culture_group = african
				culture_group = cushitic
				culture_group = sudanese
			}
				NOT = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				owner = {
					alliance_with = ROOT
				}		
				}							
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_slaves_taken
				duration =  3650 
				}			
		}	
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.655.a

# Privateer Enslavement south_asian
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_colonial_events.801
	title = cosmo_colonial_events.801.t
	desc = cosmo_colonial_events.801.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SLAVE_TRADE_eventPicture
	goto = enslavedprovince
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cxslavery_ideas_active
			has_country_flag = cslavery_ideas_active			
		}
		any_known_country = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement
		NOT = {
			war_with = ROOT 
		}
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			random_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
						has_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement
					NOT = {
						war_with = ROOT 
					}
					}
					
				}
				save_event_target_as = enslavedprovince
				owner = { clr_country_flag = cosmo_devastation_enslavement }
			}	
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_colonial_events.801.a 	
	}	
}
